    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));

    if (!item) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var row = item.querySelector(':scope > button') || item;
    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}
})();</script></div></div></div></div></div></nav><main id="content-container" class="lg:flex-1 lg:min-w-0"><script @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Authorization" data-page-href="/specification/2025-11-25/basic/authorization" id="content"><div id="enable-section-numbers"></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="introduction"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#introduction" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Introduction</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="purpose-and-scope"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#purpose-and-scope" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Purpose and Scope</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The Model Context Protocol provides authorization capabilities at the transport level,
enabling MCP clients to make requests to restricted MCP servers on behalf of resource
owners. This specification defines the authorization flow for HTTP-based transports.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="protocol-requirements"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#protocol-requirements" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Protocol Requirements</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Authorization is <strong>OPTIONAL</strong> for MCP implementations. When supported:</span>
<ul>
<li>Implementations using an HTTP-based transport <strong>SHOULD</strong> conform to this specification.</li>
<li>Implementations using an STDIO transport <strong>SHOULD NOT</strong> follow this specification, and
instead retrieve credentials from the environment.</li>
<li>Implementations using alternative transports <strong>MUST</strong> follow established security best
practices for their protocol.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="standards-compliance"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#standards-compliance" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Standards Compliance</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This authorization mechanism is based on established specifications listed below, but
implements a selected subset of their features to ensure security and interoperability
while maintaining simplicity:</span>
<ul>
<li>OAuth 2.1 IETF DRAFT (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13</a>)</li>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata
(<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8414" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC8414</a>)</li>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration Protocol
(<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7591" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC7591</a>)</li>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728</a>)</li>
<li>OAuth Client ID Metadata Documents (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="roles"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#roles" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Roles</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">A protected <em>MCP server</em> acts as an <a href="https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13.html#name-roles" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 resource server</a>,
capable of accepting and responding to protected resource requests using access tokens.</span>
<span data-as="p">An <em>MCP client</em> acts as an <a href="https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13.html#name-roles" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 client</a>,
making protected resource requests on behalf of a resource owner.</span>
<span data-as="p">The <em>authorization server</em> is responsible for interacting with the user (if necessary) and issuing access tokens for use at the MCP server.
The implementation details of the authorization server are beyond the scope of this specification. It may be hosted with the
resource server or a separate entity. The <a href="#authorization-server-discovery" class="link">Authorization Server Discovery section</a>
specifies how an MCP server indicates the location of its corresponding authorization server to a client.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="overview"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#overview" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Overview</span></h2>
<ol>
<li>
<span data-as="p">Authorization servers <strong>MUST</strong> implement OAuth 2.1 with appropriate security
measures for both confidential and public clients.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">Authorization servers and MCP clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> support OAuth Client ID Metadata Documents
(<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00</a>).</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">Authorization servers and MCP clients <strong>MAY</strong> support the OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration
Protocol (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7591" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC7591</a>).</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> implement OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728</a>).
MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> use OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata for authorization server discovery.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p">MCP authorization servers <strong>MUST</strong> provide at least one of the following discovery mechanisms:</span>
<ul>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8414" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC8414</a>)</li>
<li><a href="https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-discovery-1_0.html" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0</a></li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> support both discovery mechanisms to obtain the information required to interact with the authorization server.</span>
</li>
</ol>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authorization-server-discovery"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#authorization-server-discovery" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Authorization Server Discovery</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">This section describes the mechanisms by which MCP servers advertise their associated
authorization servers to MCP clients, as well as the discovery process through which MCP
clients can determine authorization server endpoints and supported capabilities.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authorization-server-location"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#authorization-server-location" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Authorization Server Location</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> implement the OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728</a>)
specification to indicate the locations of authorization servers. The Protected Resource Metadata document returned by the MCP server <strong>MUST</strong> include
the <code>authorization_servers</code> field containing at least one authorization server.</span>
<span data-as="p">The specific use of <code>authorization_servers</code> is beyond the scope of this specification; implementers should consult
OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata (<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728</a>) for
guidance on implementation details.</span>
<span data-as="p">Implementors should note that Protected Resource Metadata documents can define multiple authorization servers. The responsibility for selecting which authorization server to use lies with the MCP client, following the guidelines specified in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-authorization-servers" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728 Section 7.6 “Authorization Servers”</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="protected-resource-metadata-discovery-requirements"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#protected-resource-metadata-discovery-requirements" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Protected Resource Metadata Discovery Requirements</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> implement one of the following discovery mechanisms to provide authorization server location information to MCP clients:</span>
<ol>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>WWW-Authenticate Header</strong>: Include the resource metadata URL in the <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> HTTP header under <code>resource_metadata</code> when returning <code>401 Unauthorized</code> responses, as described in <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728#name-www-authenticate-response" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728 Section 5.1</a>.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Well-Known URI</strong>: Serve metadata at a well-known URI as specified in <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC9728</a>. This can be either:</span>
<ul>
<li>At the path of the server’s MCP endpoint: <code>https://example.com/public/mcp</code> could host metadata at <code>https://example.com/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource/public/mcp</code></li>
<li>At the root: <code>https://example.com/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> support both discovery mechanisms and use the resource metadata URL from the parsed <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> headers when present; otherwise, they <strong>MUST</strong> fall back to constructing and requesting the well-known URIs in the order listed above.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> include a <code>scope</code> parameter in the <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> header as defined in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750#section-3" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 6750 Section 3</a>
to indicate the scopes required for accessing the resource. This provides clients with immediate
guidance on the appropriate scopes to request during authorization,
following the principle of least privilege and preventing clients from requesting excessive permissions.</span>
<span data-as="p">The scopes included in the <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> challenge <strong>MAY</strong> match <code>scopes_supported</code>, be a subset
or superset of it, or an alternative collection that is neither a strict subset nor
superset. Clients <strong>MUST NOT</strong> assume any particular set relationship between the challenged
scope set and <code>scopes_supported</code>. Clients <strong>MUST</strong> treat the scopes provided in the
challenge as authoritative for satisfying the current request. Servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> strive for
consistency in how they construct scope sets but they are not required to surface every dynamically
issued scope through <code>scopes_supported</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Example 401 response with scope guidance:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default dark-plus" style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="http"><code language="http" numberOfLines="3"><span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">HTTP</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">/</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8">1.1</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#B5CEA8"> 401</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> Unauthorized</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">WWW-Authenticate</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> Bearer resource_metadata=&quot;https://mcp.example.com/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource&quot;,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">                         scope=&quot;files:read&quot;</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> be able to parse <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> headers and respond appropriately to <code>HTTP 401 Unauthorized</code> responses from the MCP server.</span>
<span data-as="p">If the <code>scope</code> parameter is absent, clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> apply the fallback behavior defined in the <a href="#scope-selection-strategy" class="link">Scope Selection Strategy</a> section.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authorization-server-metadata-discovery"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#authorization-server-metadata-discovery" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Authorization Server Metadata Discovery</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To handle different issuer URL formats and ensure interoperability with both OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata and OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0 specifications, MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> attempt multiple well-known endpoints when discovering authorization server metadata.</span>
<span data-as="p">The discovery approach is based on <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8414#section-3.1" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC8414 Section 3.1 “Authorization Server Metadata Request”</a> for OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata discovery and <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc8414#section-5" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC8414 Section 5 “Compatibility Notes”</a> for OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0 interoperability.</span>
<span data-as="p">For issuer URLs with path components (e.g., <code>https://auth.example.com/tenant1</code>), clients <strong>MUST</strong> try endpoints in the following priority order:</span>
<ol>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata with path insertion: <code>https://auth.example.com/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server/tenant1</code></li>
<li>OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0 with path insertion: <code>https://auth.example.com/.well-known/openid-configuration/tenant1</code></li>
<li>OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0 path appending: <code>https://auth.example.com/tenant1/.well-known/openid-configuration</code></li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">For issuer URLs without path components (e.g., <code>https://auth.example.com</code>), clients <strong>MUST</strong> try:</span>
<ol>
<li>OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata: <code>https://auth.example.com/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server</code></li>
<li>OpenID Connect Discovery 1.0: <code>https://auth.example.com/.well-known/openid-configuration</code></li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authorization-server-discovery-sequence-diagram"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#authorization-server-discovery-sequence-diagram" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Authorization Server Discovery Sequence Diagram</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The following diagram outlines an example flow:</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="client-registration-approaches"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#client-registration-approaches" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Client Registration Approaches</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MCP supports three client registration mechanisms. Choose based on your scenario:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Client ID Metadata Documents</strong>: When client and server have no prior relationship (most common)</li>
<li><strong>Pre-registration</strong>: When client and server have an existing relationship</li>
<li><strong>Dynamic Client Registration</strong>: For backwards compatibility or specific requirements</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Clients supporting all options <strong>SHOULD</strong> follow the following priority order:</span>
<ol>
<li>Use pre-registered client information for the server if the client has it available</li>
<li>Use Client ID Metadata Documents if the Authorization Server indicates if the server supports it (via <code>client_id_metadata_document_supported</code> in OAuth Authorization Server Metadata)</li>
<li>Use Dynamic Client Registration as a fallback if the Authorization Server supports it (via <code>registration_endpoint</code> in OAuth Authorization Server Metadata)</li>
<li>Prompt the user to enter the client information if no other option is available</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="client-id-metadata-documents"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#client-id-metadata-documents" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Client ID Metadata Documents</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients and authorization servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> support OAuth Client ID Metadata Documents as specified in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth Client ID Metadata Document</a>.
This approach enables clients to use HTTPS URLs as client identifiers, where the URL points to a JSON document
containing client metadata. This addresses the common MCP scenario where servers and clients have
no pre-existing relationship.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="implementation-requirements"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#implementation-requirements" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Implementation Requirements</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">MCP implementations supporting Client ID Metadata Documents <strong>MUST</strong> follow the requirements specified in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth Client ID Metadata Document</a>.
Key requirements include:</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>For MCP Clients:</strong></span>
<ul>
<li>Clients <strong>MUST</strong> host their metadata document at an HTTPS URL following RFC requirements</li>
<li>The <code>client_id</code> URL <strong>MUST</strong> use the “https” scheme and contain a path component, e.g. <code>https://example.com/client.json</code></li>
<li>The metadata document <strong>MUST</strong> include at least the following properties: <code>client_id</code>, <code>client_name</code>, <code>redirect_uris</code></li>
<li>Clients <strong>MUST</strong> ensure the <code>client_id</code> value in the metadata matches the document URL exactly</li>
<li>Clients <strong>MAY</strong> use <code>private_key_jwt</code> for client authentication (e.g., for requests to the token endpoint) with appropriate JWKS configuration as described in <a href="https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00.html#section-6.2" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Section 6.2 of Client ID Metadata Document</a></li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p"><strong>For Authorization Servers:</strong></span>
<ul>
<li><strong>SHOULD</strong> fetch metadata documents when encountering URL-formatted client_ids</li>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> validate that the fetched document’s <code>client_id</code> matches the URL exactly</li>
<li><strong>SHOULD</strong> cache metadata respecting HTTP cache headers</li>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> validate redirect URIs presented in an authorization request against those in the metadata document</li>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> validate the document structure is valid JSON and contains required fields</li>
<li><strong>SHOULD</strong> follow the security considerations in <a href="https://www.ietf.org/archive/id/draft-ietf-oauth-client-id-metadata-document-00.html#section-6" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Section 6 of Client ID Metadata Document</a></li>
</ul>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="example-metadata-document"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#example-metadata-document" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Example Metadata Document</span></h4>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="client-id-metadata-documents-flow"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#client-id-metadata-documents-flow" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Client ID Metadata Documents Flow</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">The following diagram illustrates the complete flow when using Client ID Metadata Documents:</span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="discovery"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#discovery" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Discovery</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">Authorization servers advertise that they support clients using Client ID Metadata Documents by including the following property in their OAuth Authorization Server metadata:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="3" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_fajt5dd7av5tcd74t9snpfiutb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md [.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent dark:[.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent" id="code-snippet-feedback-button" aria-label="Report incorrect code"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 shrink-0 text-gray-400 group-hover/code-snippet-feedback-button:text-gray-500 dark:text-white/40 dark:group-hover/code-snippet-feedback-button:text-white/60"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Report"></use></svg></button></div><div class="code-block-copy-button z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_fijt5dd7av5tcd74t9snpfiutb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md <span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;client_id_metadata_document_supported&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> check for this capability and <strong>MAY</strong> fall back to Dynamic Client Registration
or pre-registration if unavailable.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="preregistration"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#preregistration" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Preregistration</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> support an option for static client credentials such as those supplied by a preregistration flow. This could be:</span>
<ol>
<li>Hardcode a client ID (and, if applicable, client credentials) specifically for the MCP client to use when
interacting with that authorization server, or</li>
<li>Present a UI to users that allows them to enter these details, after registering an
OAuth client themselves (e.g., through a configuration interface hosted by the
server).</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="dynamic-client-registration"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#dynamic-client-registration" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Dynamic Client Registration</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients and authorization servers <strong>MAY</strong> support the
OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration Protocol <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc7591" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC7591</a>
to allow MCP clients to obtain OAuth client IDs without user interaction.
This option is included for backwards compatibility with earlier versions of the MCP authorization spec.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="scope-selection-strategy"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#scope-selection-strategy" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Scope Selection Strategy</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">When implementing authorization flows, MCP clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> follow the principle of least privilege by requesting
only the scopes necessary for their intended operations. During the initial authorization handshake, MCP clients
<strong>SHOULD</strong> follow this priority order for scope selection:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>Use <code>scope</code> parameter</strong> from the initial <code>WWW-Authenticate</code> header in the 401 response, if provided</li>
<li><strong>If <code>scope</code> is not available</strong>, use all scopes defined in <code>scopes_supported</code> from the Protected Resource Metadata document, omitting the <code>scope</code> parameter if <code>scopes_supported</code> is undefined.</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">This approach accommodates the general-purpose nature of MCP clients, which typically lack domain-specific knowledge to make informed decisions about individual scope selection. Requesting all available scopes allows the authorization server and end-user to determine appropriate permissions during the consent process.</span>
<span data-as="p">This approach minimizes user friction while following the principle of least privilege.
The <code>scopes_supported</code> field is intended to represent the minimal set of scopes necessary
for basic functionality (see <a class="link" href="/specification/2025-11-25/basic/security_best_practices#scope-minimization">Scope Minimization</a>),
with additional scopes requested incrementally through the step-up authorization flow steps
described in the <a href="#scope-challenge-handling" class="link">Scope Challenge Handling</a> section.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authorization-flow-steps"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#authorization-flow-steps" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Authorization Flow Steps</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The complete Authorization flow proceeds as follows:</span>
<!--$!--><template data-dgst="BAILOUT_TO_CLIENT_SIDE_RENDERING"></template><!--/$-->
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="resource-parameter-implementation"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#resource-parameter-implementation" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Resource Parameter Implementation</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> implement Resource Indicators for OAuth 2.0 as defined in <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8707.html" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 8707</a>
to explicitly specify the target resource for which the token is being requested. The <code>resource</code> parameter:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> be included in both authorization requests and token requests.</li>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> identify the MCP server that the client intends to use the token with.</li>
<li><strong>MUST</strong> use the canonical URI of the MCP server as defined in <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8707.html#name-access-token-request" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 8707 Section 2</a>.</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="canonical-server-uri"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#canonical-server-uri" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Canonical Server URI</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">For the purposes of this specification, the canonical URI of an MCP server is defined as the resource identifier as specified in
<a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8707.html#section-2" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 8707 Section 2</a> and aligns with the <code>resource</code> parameter in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 9728</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>SHOULD</strong> provide the most specific URI that they can for the MCP server they intend to access, following the guidance in <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8707" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 8707</a>. While the canonical form uses lowercase scheme and host components, implementations <strong>SHOULD</strong> accept uppercase scheme and host components for robustness and interoperability.</span>
<span data-as="p">Examples of valid canonical URIs:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>https://mcp.example.com/mcp</code></li>
<li><code>https://mcp.example.com</code></li>
<li><code>https://mcp.example.com:8443</code></li>
<li><code>https://mcp.example.com/server/mcp</code> (when path component is necessary to identify individual MCP server)</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Examples of invalid canonical URIs:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>mcp.example.com</code> (missing scheme)</li>
<li><code>https://mcp.example.com#fragment</code> (contains fragment)</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Note:</strong> While both <code>https://mcp.example.com/</code> (with trailing slash) and <code>https://mcp.example.com</code> (without trailing slash) are technically valid absolute URIs according to <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc3986" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 3986</a>, implementations <strong>SHOULD</strong> consistently use the form without the trailing slash for better interoperability unless the trailing slash is semantically significant for the specific resource.</span>
</blockquote>
<span data-as="p">For example, if accessing an MCP server at <code>https://mcp.example.com</code>, the authorization request would include:</span>

<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST</strong> send this parameter regardless of whether authorization servers support it.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="access-token-usage"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#access-token-usage" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Access Token Usage</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="token-requirements"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#token-requirements" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Token Requirements</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Access token handling when making requests to MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> conform to the requirements defined in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13#section-5" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 Section 5 “Resource Requests”</a>.
Specifically:</span>
<ol>
<li>MCP client <strong>MUST</strong> use the Authorization request header field defined in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13#section-5.1.1" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 Section 5.1.1</a>:</li>
</ol>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 language="text" numberOfLines="2"><span class="line"><span>Authorization: Bearer &lt;access-token&gt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Note that authorization <strong>MUST</strong> be included in every HTTP request from client to server,
even if they are part of the same logical session.</span>
<ol start="2">
<li>Access tokens <strong>MUST NOT</strong> be included in the URI query string</li>
</ol>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="token-handling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#token-handling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Token Handling</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers, acting in their role as an OAuth 2.1 resource server, <strong>MUST</strong> validate access tokens as described in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13#section-5.2" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 Section 5.2</a>.
MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> validate that access tokens were issued specifically for them as the intended audience,
according to <a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8707.html#section-2" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 8707 Section 2</a>.
If validation fails, servers <strong>MUST</strong> respond according to
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13#section-5.3" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 Section 5.3</a>
error handling requirements. Invalid or expired tokens <strong>MUST</strong> receive a HTTP 401
response.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP clients <strong>MUST NOT</strong> send tokens to the MCP server other than ones issued by the MCP server’s authorization server.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MUST</strong> only accept tokens that are valid for use with their
own resources.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers <strong>MUST NOT</strong> accept or transit any other tokens.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="error-handling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#error-handling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Error Handling</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>MUST</strong> return appropriate HTTP status codes for authorization errors:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_6n5dd7av5tcd74t9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Status Code</th><th>Description</th><th>Usage</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td data-numeric="true">401</td><td>Unauthorized</td><td>Authorization required or token invalid</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true">403</td><td>Forbidden</td><td>Invalid scopes or insufficient permissions</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true">400</td><td>Bad Request</td><td>Malformed authorization request</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="scope-challenge-handling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#scope-challenge-handling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Scope Challenge Handling</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This section covers handling insufficient scope errors during runtime operations when
a client already has a token but needs additional permissions. This follows the error
handling patterns defined in <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-ietf-oauth-v2-1-13#section-5" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">OAuth 2.1 Section 5</a>
and leverages the metadata fields from <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9728" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 9728 (OAuth 2.0 Protected Resource Metadata)</a>.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="runtime-insufficient-scope-errors"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#runtime-insufficient-scope-errors" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Runtime Insufficient Scope Errors</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">When a client makes a request with an access token with insufficient
scope during runtime operations, the server <strong>SHOULD</strong> respond with:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>HTTP 403 Forbidden</code> status code (per <a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc6750#section-3.1" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">RFC 6750 Section 3.1</a>)</li>
<li><code>WWW-Authenticate</code> header with the <code>Bearer</code> scheme and additional parameters:<!-- -->
<ul>
<li><code>error=&quot;insufficient_scope&quot;</code> - indicating the specific type of authorization failure</li>
<li><code>scope=&quot;required_scope1 required_scope2&quot;</code> - specifying the minimum scopes needed for the operation</li>
<li><code>resource_metadata</code> - the URI of the Protected Resource Metadata document (for consistency with 401 responses)</li>
<li><code>error_description</code> (optional) - human-readable description of the error</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Server Scope Management</strong>: When responding with insufficient scope errors, servers
<strong>SHOULD</strong> include the scopes needed to satisfy the current request in the <code>scope</code>
parameter.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers have flexibility in determining which scopes to include:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Minimum approach</strong>: Include the newly-required scopes for the specific operation. Include any existing granted scopes as well, if they are required, to prevent clients from losing previously granted permissions.</li>
<li><strong>Recommended approach</strong>: Include both existing relevant scopes and newly required scopes to prevent clients from losing previously granted permissions</li>
<li><strong>Extended approach</strong>: Include existing scopes, newly required scopes, and related scopes that commonly work together</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The choice depends on the server’s assessment of user experience impact and authorization friction.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> be consistent in their scope inclusion strategy to provide predictable behavior for clients.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers <strong>SHOULD</strong> consider the user experience impact when determining which scopes to include in the
response, as misconfigured scopes may require frequent user interaction.</span>
<span data-as="p">Example insufficient scope response:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">WWW-Authenticate</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> Bearer error=&quot;insufficient_scope&quot;,</span></span>
